default=[\\\"spatial\\\"],\\n                   choices=[\\\"object\\\", \\\"spatial\\\", \\\"goal\\\", \\\"long\\\", \\\"90\\\"])\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--epochs\\\", type=int, default=50)\\n p.add_argument(\\\"--output_dir\\\", type=str,\\n                   default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/crm/\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--cache_dir\\\", type=str,\\n type=int, default=50)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--val_split\\\", type=float, default=0.1)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--log_every\\\", type=int, default=50)\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\n# ── Precomputation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef precompute_features(args):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Run frozen SmolVLA on all demo timesteps, save features to disk.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from smolvla_wrapper import SmolVLAWrapper\\n\\n    benchmarks_to_run = []\\n    for bm in args.benchmark:\\n        cache_dir = Path(args.cache_dir) / bm\\n        if (cache_dir / (B, 960)\\n            k_samp = batch[\\\"k_samples\\\"].to(device)             # (B, K, H, D)\\n            expert = batch[\\\"expert_action\\\"].to(device)         # (B, H, D)\\n            expert_flat = expert.reshape(expert.shape[0], -1)  # (B, 30)\\n\\n            # CGS\\n            uf, ma = cgs.compute_features_batch(k_samp)\\n            uf = uf.to(device)\\n            ma = ma.to(device)\\n\\n            # CRM forward\\n            refined = crm(ma, uf, obs_feat)\\n            loss = loss_fn(refined, expert_flat)\\n\\n            # ── Validate ──\\n        crm.eval()\\n        val_loss = 0.0\\n        n_val_batches = 0\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            for batch in val_loader:\\n                obs_feat = batch[\\\"obs_features\\\"].to(device)\\n                k_samp = batch[\\\"k_samples\\\"].to(device)\\n                expert = batch[\\\"expert_action\\\"].to(device)\\n                expert_flat = expert.reshape(expert.shape[0], -1)\\n\\n                uf, ma = cgs.compute_features_batch(k_samp)\\n                uf, ma = uf.to(device), ma.to(device)\\n\\n                refined = crm(ma, uf, obs_feat)\\n                val_loss += loss_fn(refined, expert_flat).item()\\n                n_val_batches += 1\\n\\n        avg_val_loss = val_loss / max(n_val_batches, 1)\\n\\n        print(f\\\"Epoch {epoch}/{args.epochs}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"train_loss={avg_train_loss:.6f}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"val_loss={avg_val_loss:.6f}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"lr={scheduler.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e}\\\")\\n\\n        metrics = {\\\"train_loss\\\": avg_train_loss, \\\"val_loss\\\": avg_val_loss}\\n        ckpt_mgr.save(crm, optimizer, epoch, metrics)\\n\\n        if avg_val_loss < best_val_loss:\\n            best_val_loss = avg_val_loss\\n            torch.save(crm.state_dict(),\\n                       Path(args.output_dir) / \\\"crm_best.pt\\\")\\n            print(f\\\"  → New best val_loss: {best_val_loss:.6f}\\\")\\n\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n[train] Done. K-sample flow-matching generation with shared KV cache\\\\n  3. LIBERO data preprocessing (image, language, state → SmolVLA inputs)\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nimport math\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport torch.nn.functional as F\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nfrom PIL import Image\\\\nfrom torchvision import transforms as T\\\\n\\\\nfrom lerobot.policies.smolvla.modeling_smolvla import (\\\\n    SmolVLAPolicy,\\\\n    VLAFlowMatching,\\\\n    make_att_2d_masks,\\\\n    resize_with_pad,\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass SmolVLAWrapper:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Frozen SmolVLA for obs_features and K-sample generation.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, pretrained_path: str = \\\\\\\"lerobot/smolvla_base\\\\\\\",\\\\n                 device: str = \\\\\\\"cuda\\\\\\\"):\\\\n        self.device = torch.device(device)\\\\n        self.policy = SmolVLAPolicy.from_pretrained(pretrained_path)\\\\n        self.policy.to(self.device)\\\\n        self.policy.eval()\\\\n        for p in self.policy.parameters():\\\\n            p.requires_grad = False\\\\n\\\\n        self.flow: VLAFlowMatching = self.policy.model\\\\n        self.processor = self.flow.vlm_with_expert.processor\\\\n        self.tokenizer = self.processor.tokenizer\\\\n        self.config = self.policy.config\\\\n\\\\n        self.vlm_hidden_size = self.flow.vlm_with_expert.config.text_config.hidden_size  # 960\\\\n        self.img_transform = T.Compose([\\\\n            T.ToTensor(),  # HWC uint8 → CHW float [0,1]\\\\n        ])\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def preprocess_obs(self, agentview_rgb: np.ndarray,\\\\n                       task_name: str,\\\\n                       joint_states: np.ndarray,\\\\n                       eye_in_hand_rgb: np.ndarray | None = None):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Convert raw LIBERO observation into SmolVLA-ready tensors.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            agentview_rgb: (H, W, 3) uint8\\\\n            task_name: task description string\\\\n            joint_states: (7,) float64\\\\n            eye_in_hand_rgb: optional (H, W, 3) uint8\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            images: list of (1, C, H, W) float tensors\\\\n            img_masks: list of (1,) bool tensors\\\\n            lang_tokens: (1, L) long tensor\\\\n            lang_masks: (1, L) bool tensor\\\\n            state: (1, max_state_dim) float tensor\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        dev = self.device\\\\n\\\\n        imgs = [self.img_transform(agentview_rgb).unsqueeze(0).to(dev)]\\\\n        img_masks = [torch.ones(1, dtype=torch.bool, device=dev)]\\\\n        if eye_in_hand_rgb is not None:\\\\n            imgs.append(self.img_transform(eye_in_hand_rgb).unsqueeze(0).to(dev))\\\\n            img_masks.append(torch.ones(1, dtype=torch.bool, device=dev))\\\\n\\\\n        tokens = self.tokenizer(\\\\n            task_name,\\\\n            return_tensors=\\\\\\\"pt\\\\\\\",\\\\n            padding=\\\\\\\"max_length\\\\\\\",\\\\n            max_length=self.config.tokenizer_max_length,\\\\n            truncation=True,\\\\n        )\\\\n        lang_tokens = tokens[\\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\"].to(dev)\\\\n        lang_masks = tokens[\\\\\\\"attention_mask\\\\\\\"].to(dev).bool()\\\\n\\\\n        state = torch.zeros(1, self.config.max_state_dim, device=dev, dtype=torch.float32)\\\\n        state[0, :joint_states.shape[0]] = torch.tensor(joint_states, dtype=torch.float32)\\\\n\\\\n        return imgs, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def encode_prefix(self, images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Encode observation prefix through VLM, return obs_features and KV cache.\\\\n\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            obs_features: (B, 960)\\\\n            past_key_values: KV cache for denoising\\\\n            prefix_pad_masks: (B, seq_len) for denoising attention\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        prefix_embs, prefix_pad_masks, prefix_att_masks = self.flow.embed_prefix(\\\\n            images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state=state\\\\n        )\\\\n        prefix_att_2d_masks = make_att_2d_masks(prefix_pad_masks, prefix_att_masks)\\\\n        prefix_position_ids = torch.cumsum(prefix_pad_masks, dim=1) - 1\\\\n\\\\n        outputs_embeds, past_key_values = self.flow.vlm_with_expert.forward(\\\\n            attention_mask=prefix_att_2d_masks,\\\\n            position_ids=prefix_position_ids,\\\\n            past_key_values=None,\\\\n            inputs_embeds=[prefix_embs, None],\\\\n            use_cache=self.config.use_cache,\\\\n            fill_kv_cache=True,\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n        vlm_hidden = outputs_embeds[0]  # (B, seq_len, 960)\\\\n        mask = prefix_pad_masks.unsqueeze(-1).float()\\\\n        obs_features = (vlm_hidden.float() * mask).sum(1) / mask.sum(1).clamp(min=1)\\\\n\\\\n        return obs_features, past_key_values, prefix_pad_masks\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def generate_k_samples(self, past_key_values, prefix_pad_masks,\\\\n                           K: int = 10) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Generate K action samples using cached prefix KV.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            past_key_values: KV cache from encode_prefix\\\\n            prefix_pad_masks: (B, seq_len)\\\\n            K: number of samples\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            samples: (B, K, chunk_size, max_action_dim)\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        B = prefix_pad_masks.shape[0]\\\\n        chunk_size = self.config.chunk_size\\\\n        max_act_dim = self.config.max_action_dim\\\\n        num_steps = self.config.num_steps\\\\n        dt = -1.0 / num_steps\\\\n\\\\n        all_samples = []\\\\n        for _ in range(K):\\\\n            noise = self.flow.sample_noise((B, chunk_size, max_act_dim), self.device)\\\\n            x_t = noise\\\\n\\\\n            for step in range(num_steps):\\\\n                time_val = 1.0 + step * dt\\\\n                time_tensor = torch.full((B,), time_val, dtype=torch.float32,\\\\n                                         device=self.device)\\\\n                v_t = self.flow.denoise_step(\\\\n                    x_t=x_t,\\\\n                    prefix_pad_masks=prefix_pad_masks,\\\\n                    past_key_values=past_key_values,\\\\n                    timestep=time_tensor,\\\\n                )\\\\n                x_t = x_t + dt * v_t\\\\n\\\\n            all_samples.append(x_t)\\\\n\\\\n        return torch.stack(all_samples, dim=1)  # (B, K, chunk_size, max_action_dim)\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def get_obs_features_and_samples(self, agentview_rgb, task_name, joint_states,\\\\n                                     eye_in_hand_rgb=None, K=10):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        End-to-end: raw obs → obs_features (960D) + K samples.\\\\n\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            obs_features: (1, 960)\\\\n            samples: (1, K, chunk_size, max_action_dim)\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        imgs, img_masks, lt, lm, state = self.preprocess_obs(\\\\n            agentview_rgb, task_name, joint_states, eye_in_hand_rgb\\\\n        )\\\\n        obs_feat, kv_cache, pad_masks = self.encode_prefix(\\\\n            imgs, img_masks, lt, lm, state\\\\n        )\\\\n        samples = self.generate_k_samples(kv_cache, pad_masks, K=K)\\\\n        return obs_feat, samples\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01M3x2igS1SecMNwuyNZ81Qd","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nConformalized Gaussian Scoring (CGS) module.\\\\n\\\\nInput: K flow-matching samples, shape (K, H_eff, D) where D=6, H_eff=5.\\\\n       Accepts (K, chunk_size, D') and auto-truncates to (K, H_eff, action_dim).\\\\n\\\\nOutput:\\\\n  - uncertainty_features: 40D tensor (per_dof_std 30 + conformal_radius 5 + log_volume 5)\\\\n  - mean_action: 30D tensor (K samples mean, flattened from (H_eff, D))\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nfrom sklearn.covariance import LedoitWolf\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass ConformilizedGaussianScoring:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Compute structured uncertainty features from K flow-matching samples.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, K: int = 10, horizon: int = 5, action_dim: int = 6,\\\\n                 alpha: float = 0.1, chunk_size: int = 50):\\\\n        self.K = K\\\\n        self.horizon = horizon       # H_eff = 5\\\\n        self.action_dim = action_dim  # 6 DOF (no gripper)\\\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\\\n        self.chunk_size = chunk_size\\\\n\\\\n    def compute_features(self, samples: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            samples: (K, T, D') where T >= H_eff and D' >= action_dim.\\\\n                     Auto-truncates to (K, H_eff, action_dim).\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            uncertainty_features: (40,) — [per_dof_std(30), conformal_radius(5), log_volume(5)]\\\\n            mean_action: (30,) — flattened mean over K samples\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if samples.shape[1] > self.horizon:\\\\n            samples = samples[:, :self.horizon, :]\\\\n        if samples.shape[2] > self.action_dim:\\\\n            samples = samples[:, :, :self.action_dim]\\\\n\\\\n        assert samples.shape == (self.K, self.horizon, self.action_dim), \\\\\\\\\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"Expected ({self.K}, {self.horizon}, {self.action_dim}), got {samples.shape}\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n        mean_action = samples.mean(dim=0)  # (H, D)\\\\n\\\\n        per_dof_stds = []\\\\n        conformal_radii = []\\\\n        log_volumes = []\\\\n\\\\n        for h in range(self.horizon):\\\\n            step_samples = samples[:, h, :]  # (K, D)\\\\n            cov_matrix = self._ledoit_wolf_cov(step_samples)  # (D, D)\\\\n\\\\n            stds = torch.sqrt(torch.diag(cov_matrix).clamp(min=1e-10))\\\\n            per_dof_stds.append(stds)\\\\n\\\\n            q_h = self._conformal_radius(step_samples, mean_action[h], cov_matrix)\\\\n            conformal_radii.append(q_h)\\\\n\\\\n            sign, logabsdet = torch.linalg.slogdet(cov_matrix)\\\\n            log_vol = 0.5 * logabsdet\\\\n            log_volumes.append(log_vol)\\\\n\\\\n        # 6*5=30 + 5 + 5 = 40D\\\\n        uncertainty_features = torch.cat([\\\\n            torch.cat(per_dof_stds),       # (30,)\\\\n            torch.stack(conformal_radii),   # (5,)\\\\n            torch.stack(log_volumes),       # (5,)\\\\n        ])\\\\n\\\\n        return uncertainty_features, mean_action.flatten()\\\\n\\\\n    def compute_features_batch(self, samples: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Batch version: process B sets of K samples.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            samples: (B, K, T, D') — auto-truncates to (B, K, H_eff, action_dim)\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            uncertainty_features: (B, 40)\\\\n            mean_action: (B, 30)\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        B = samples.shape[0]\\\\n        all_uf = []\\\\n        all_ma = []\\\\n        for b in range(B):\\\\n            uf, ma = self.compute_features(samples[b])\\\\n            all_uf.append(uf)\\\\n            all_ma.append(ma)\\\\n        return torch.stack(all_uf), torch.stack(all_ma)\\\\n\\\\n    def _ledoit_wolf_cov(self, samples: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        samples_np = samples.detach().cpu().numpy()\\\\n        lw = LedoitWolf().fit(samples_np)\\\\n        return torch.tensor(lw.covariance_, dtype=samples.dtype, device=samples.device)\\\\n\\\\n    def _conformal_radius(self, samples: torch.Tensor, mean: torch.Tensor,\\\\n                          cov: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        diff = samples - mean.unsqueeze(0)\\\\n        cov_inv = torch.linalg.inv(cov + 1e-6 * torch.eye(cov.shape[0], device=cov.device))\\\\n        mahal = torch.sqrt((diff @ cov_inv * diff).sum(dim=1).clamp(min=0))\\\\n\\\\n        q_idx = int(np.ceil((1 - self.alpha) * (self.K + 1))) - 1\\\\n        q_idx = min(q_idx, self.K - 1)\\\\n        q_h = torch.sort(mahal)[0][q_idx]\\\\n        return q_h\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\"\\\", \\\"return_code\\\": 0}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {\\\"elapsed_seconds\\\": 0.3}}\"}"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0672f7e2-0e43-4c1f-a727-2021f7e06766","uuid":"057c4f55-6339-4324-bc82-1aefb50a5948","timestamp":"2026-05-13T06:37:20.297Z","tool_use_result":{"content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": LedoitWolf\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass ConformilizedGaussianScoring:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Compute structured uncertainty features from K flow-matching samples.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, K: int = 10, horizon: int = 5, action_dim: int = 6,\\\\n                 alpha: float = 0.1, chunk_size: int = 50):\\\\n        self.K = K\\\\n        self.horizon = horizon       # H_eff = 5\\\\n        self.action_dim = action_dim  # 6 DOF (no gripper)\\\\n        self.alpha = alpha\\\\n        self.chunk_size = chunk_size\\\\n\\\\n    def compute_features(self, samples: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            samples: (K, T, D') where T >= H_eff and D' >= action_dim.\\\\n                     Auto-truncates to (K, H_eff, action_dim).\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            uncertainty_features: (40,) — [per_dof_std(30), conformal_radius(5), log_volume(5)]\\\\n            mean_action: (30,) — flattened mean over K samples\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if samples.shape[1] > self.horizon:\\\\n            samples = samples[:, :self.horizon, :]\\\\n        if samples.shape[2] > self.action_dim:\\\\n            samples = samples[:, :, :self.action_dim]\\\\n\\\\n        assert samples.shape == (self.K, self.horizon, self.action_dim), \\\\\\\\\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"Expected ({self.K}, {self.horizon}, {self.action_dim}), got {samples.shape}\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n        mean_action = samples.mean(dim=0)  # (H, D)\\\\n\\\\n        per_dof_stds = []\\\\n        conformal_radii = []\\\\n        log_volumes = []\\\\n\\\\n        for h in range(self.horizon):\\\\n            step_samples = samples[:, h, :]  # (K, D)\\\\n            cov_matrix = self._ledoit_wolf_cov(step_samples)  # (D, D)\\\\n\\\\n            stds = torch.sqrt(torch.diag(cov_matrix).clamp(min=1e-10))\\\\n            per_dof_stds.append(stds)\\\\n\\\\n            q_h = self._conformal_radius(step_samples, mean_action[h], cov_matrix)\\\\n            conformal_radii.append(q_h)\\\\n\\\\n            sign, logabsdet = torch.linalg.slogdet(cov_matrix)\\\\n            log_vol = 0.5 * logabsdet\\\\n            log_volumes.append(log_vol)\\\\n\\\\n        # 6*5=30 + 5 + 5 = 40D\\\\n        uncertainty_features = torch.cat([\\\\n            torch.cat(per_dof_stds), mean_action.flatten()\\\\n\\\\n    def compute_features_batch(self, samples: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Batch version: process B sets of K samples.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            samples: (B, K, T, D') — auto-truncates to (B, K, H_eff, action_dim)\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            uncertainty_features: (B, 40)\\\\n            mean_action: (B, 30)\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        B = samples.shape[0]\\\\n        all_uf = []\\\\n        all_ma = []\\\\n        for b in range(B):\\\\n            uf, ma = self.compute_features(samples[b])\\\\n            all_uf.append(uf)\\\\n            all_ma.append(ma)\\\\n        return torch.stack(all_uf), torch.stack(all_ma)\\\\n\\\\n    def _ledoit_wolf_cov(self, samples: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        samples_np = samples.detach().cpu().numpy()\\\\n        lw = LedoitWolf().fit(samples_np)\\\\n        return torch.tensor(lw.covariance_, dtype=samples.dtype, device=samples.device)\\\\n\\\\n    def _conformal_radius(self, samples: torch.Tensor, mean: torch.Tensor,\\\\n                          cov: Saved {len(samples)} samples to {cache_dir}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _save_cache(cache_dir, obs_feat, k_samp, expert_act, partial=False):\\\\n    suffix = \\\\\\\"_partial\\\\\\\" if partial else \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    torch.save(torch.stack(obs_feat), cache_dir / f\\\\\\\"obs_features{suffix}.pt\\\\\\\")\\\\n    torch.save(torch.stack(k_samp), cache_dir / f\\\\\\\"k_samples{suffix}.pt\\\\\\\")\\\\n    torch.save(torch.stack(expert_act), cache_dir / f\\\\\\\"expert_actions{suffix}.pt\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ── Dataset ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\\\n\\\\nclass CRMDataset(Dataset):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Dataset of precomputed (obs_features, k_samples, expert_action) tuples.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, obs_features, k_samples, expert_actions):\\\\n        self.obs_features = obs_features    # (N, 960)\\\\n        self.k_samples = k_samples          # (N, K, H_eff, ACTION_DIM)\\\\n        self.expert_actions = expert_actions  # (N, H_eff, ACTION_DIM)\\\\n\\\\n    def __len__(self):\\\\n        return self.obs_features.shape[0]\\\\n\\\\n    def __getitem__(self, idx):\\\\n        return {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"obs_features\\\\\\\": self.obs_features[idx],\\\\n            \\\\\\\"k_samples\\\\\\\": self.k_samples[idx],\\\\n            \\\\\\\"expert_action\\\\\\\": self.expert_actions[idx],\\\\n        }\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ── Training ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\\\n\\\\ndef train(args):\\\\n    set_seed(args.seed)\\\\n    device = torch.device(\\\\\\\"cuda\\\\\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    all_obs_features = []\\\\n    all_k_samples = []\\\\n    all_expert_actions = []\\\\n    manifest = None\\\\n\\\\n    for bm in args.benchmark:\\\\n        cache_dir = Path(args.cache_dir) / bm\\\\n        manifest_path = cache_dir / \\\\\\\"manifest.json\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if not manifest_path.exists():\\\\n            raise FileNotFoundError(\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"No precomputed cache at {cache_dir}. \\\\\\\"\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"Run with --precompute first, or use --auto.\\\\\\\"\\\\n            )\\\\n        manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text())\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"[train] Loading cache ({bm}): {manifest['n_samples']} samples, \\\\\\\"\\\\n              f\\\\\\\"K={manifest['K']}, obs_dim={manifest['obs_dim']}\\\\\\\")\\\\n        all_obs_features.append(torch.load(cache_dir / \\\\\\\"obs_features.pt\\\\\\\", weights_only=True))\\\\n        all_k_samples.append(torch.load(cache_dir / \\\\\\\"k_samples.pt\\\\\\\", weights_only=True))\\\\n        all_expert_actions.append(torch.load(cache_dir / \\\\\\\"expert_actions.pt\\\\\\\", weights_only=True))\\\\n\\\\n    obs_features = torch.cat(all_obs_features)\\\\n    k_samples = torch.cat(all_k_samples)\\\\n    expert_actions = torch.cat(all_expert_actions)\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"[train] Total: {obs_features.shape[0]} samples from \\\\\\\"\\\\n          f\\\\\\\"{len(args.benchmark)} benchmark(s)\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    # Train/val split\\\\n    N = obs_features.shape[0]\\\\n    n_val = int(N * args.val_split)\\\\n    n_train = N - n_val\\\\n    perm = torch.randperm(N)\\\\n    train_idx, val_idx = perm[:n_train], perm[n_train:]\\\\n\\\\n    train_ds = CRMDataset(obs_features[train_idx], k_samples[train_idx],\\\\n                          expert_actions[train_idx])\\\\n    val_ds = CRMDataset(obs_features[val_idx], k_samples[val_idx],\\\\n                        expert_actions[val_idx])\\\\n\\\\n    train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=args.batch_size,\\\\n                              shuffle=True, num_workers=2, pin_memory=True)\\\\n    val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=args.batch_size * 2,\\\\n                            shuffle=False, num_workers=2, pin_memory=True)\\\\n\\\\n    # CGS + CRM\\\\n    cgs = ConformilizedGaussianScoring(\\\\n        K=manifest[\\\\\\\"K\\\\\\\"], horizon=H_EFF, action_dim=ACTION_DIM\\\\n    )\\\\n    crm = ConformalRefinementModule(\\\\n        action_dim=H_EFF * ACTION_DIM,  # 30\\\\n        uncertainty_dim=ACTION_DIM * H_EFF + H_EFF + H_EFF,  # 40\\\\n        obs_dim=manifest[\\\\\\\"obs_dim\\\\\\\"],\\\\n        hidden_dim=args.hidden_dim,\\\\n    ).to(device)\\\\n\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"[train] CRM parameters: {crm.count_parameters\\\\n\\\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 252 字符] ...\\\\n\\\\nmax_keep=5)\\\\n\\\\n    noise_scale = torch.linspace(\\\\n        args.aug_noise_min, args.aug_noise_max, ACTION_DIM\\\\n    ).to(device)\\\\n\\\\n    best_val_loss = float(\\\\\\\"inf\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    for epoch in range(1, args.epochs + 1):\\\\n        # ── Train ──\\\\n        crm.train()\\\\n        epoch_loss = 0.0\\\\n        n_batches = 0\\\\n\\\\n        for batch_idx, batch in enumerate(train_loader):\\\\n            obs_feat = batch[\\\\\\\"obs_features\\\\\\\"].to(device)       # (B, 960)\\\\n            k_samp = batch[\\\\\\\"k_samples\\\\\\\"].to(device)             # (B, K, H, D)\\\\n            expert = batch[\\\\\\\"expert_action\\\\\\\"].to(device)         # (B, H, D)\\\\n            expert_flat = expert.reshape(expert.shape[0], -1)  # (B, 30)\\\\n\\\\n            # CGS\\\\n            uf, ma = cgs.compute_features_batch(k_samp)\\\\n            uf = uf.to(device)\\\\n            ma = ma.to(device)\\\\n\\\\n            # CRM forward\\\\n            refined = crm(ma, uf, obs_feat)\\\\n            loss = loss_fn(refined, expert_flat)\\\\n\\\\n            # Synthetic augmentation: perturb inputs, keep clean target\\\\n            n_aug = int(obs_feat.shape[0] * args.aug_ratio)\\\\n            if n_aug > 0:\\\\n                ma_noise = noise_scale.repeat(H_EFF)\\\\n                aug_ma = ma[:n_aug] + torch.randn_like(ma[:n_aug]) * ma_noise\\\\n                aug_uf = uf[:n_aug] + torch.randn_like(uf[:n_aug]) * noise_scale.mean() * 0.1\\\\n                aug_refined = crm(aug_ma, aug_uf, obs_feat[:n_aug])\\\\n                aug_loss = loss_fn(aug_refined, expert_flat[:n_aug])\\\\n                loss = loss + 0.5 * aug_loss\\\\n\\\\n            optimizer.zero_grad()\\\\n            loss.backward()\\\\n            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(crm.parameters(), 1.0)\\\\n            optimizer.step()\\\\n\\\\n            epoch_loss += loss.item()\\\\n            n_batches += 1\\\\n\\\\n            if (batch_idx + 1) % args.log_every == 0:\\\\n                print(f\\\\\\\"  epoch {epoch} batch {batch_idx+1}/{len(train_loader)} \\\\\\\"\\\\n                      f\\\\\\\"loss={loss.item():.6f}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        scheduler.step()\\\\n        avg_train_loss = epoch_loss / max(n_batches, 1)\\\\n\\\\n        # ── Validate ──\\\\n        crm.eval()\\\\n        val_loss = 0.0\\\\n        n_val_batches = 0\\\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\\\n            for batch in val_loader:\\\\n                obs_feat = batch[\\\\\\\"obs_features\\\\\\\"].to(device)\\\\n                k_samp = batch[\\\\\\\"k_samples\\\\\\\"].to(device)\\\\n                expert = batch[\\\\\\\"expert_action\\\\\\\"].to(device)\\\\n                expert_flat = expert.reshape(expert.shape[0], -1)\\\\n\\\\n                uf, ma = cgs.compute_features_batch(k_samp)\\\\n                uf, ma = uf.to(device), ma.to(device)\\\\n\\\\n                refined = crm(ma, uf, obs_feat)\\\\n                val_loss += loss_fn(refined, expert_flat).item()\\\\n                n_val_batches += 1\\\\n\\\\n        avg_val_loss = val_loss / max(n_val_batches, 1)\\\\n\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"Epoch {epoch}/{args.epochs}  \\\\\\\"\\\\n              f\\\\\\\"train_loss={avg_train_loss:.6f}  \\\\\\\"\\\\n              f\\\\\\\"val_loss={avg_val_loss:.6f}  \\\\\\\"\\\\n              f\\\\\\\"lr={scheduler.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        metrics = {\\\\\\\"train_loss\\\\\\\": avg_train_loss, \\\\\\\"val_loss\\\\\\\": avg_val_loss}\\\\n        ckpt_mgr.save(crm, optimizer, epoch, metrics)\\\\n\\\\n        if avg_val_loss < best_val_loss:\\\\n            best_val_loss = avg_val_loss\\\\n            torch.save(crm.state_dict(),\\\\n                       Path(args.output_dir) / \\\\\\\"crm_best.pt\\\\\\\")\\\\n            print(f\\\\\\\"  → New best val_loss: {best_val_loss:.6f}\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n[train] Done. Saved {len(samples)} samples to {cache_dir}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef _save_cache(cache_dir, obs_feat, k_samp, expert_act, partial=False):\\n    suffix = \\\"_partial\\\" if partial else \\\"\\\"\\n    torch.save(torch.stack(obs_feat), cache_dir / f\\\"obs_features{suffix}.pt\\\")\\n    torch.save(torch.stack(k_samp), cache_dir / f\\\"k_samples{suffix}.pt\\\")\\n    torch.save(torch.stack(expert_act), cache_dir / f\\\"expert_actions{suffix}.pt\\\")\\n\\n\\n# ── Dataset ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\nclass CRMDataset(Dataset):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Dataset of precomputed (obs_features, k_samples, expert_action) tuples.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, obs_features, k_samples, expert_actions):\\n        self.obs_features = obs_features    # (N, 960)\\n        self.k_samples = k_samples          # (N, K, H_eff, ACTION_DIM)\\n        self.expert_actions = expert_actions  # (N, H_eff, ACTION_DIM)\\n\\n    def __len__(self):\\n        return self.obs_features.shape[0]\\n\\n    def __getitem__(self, idx):\\n        return {\\n            \\\"obs_features\\\": self.obs_features[idx],\\n            \\\"k_samples\\\": self.k_samples[idx],\\n            \\\"expert_action\\\": self.expert_actions[idx],\\n        }\\n\\n\\n# ── Training ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef train(args):\\n    set_seed(args.seed)\\n    device = torch.device(\\\"cuda\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\"cpu\\\")\\n\\n    all_obs_features = []\\n    all_k_samples = []\\n    all_expert_actions = []\\n    manifest = None\\n\\n    for bm in args.benchmark:\\n        cache_dir = Path(args.cache_dir) / bm\\n        manifest_path = cache_dir / \\\"manifest.json\\\"\\n        if not manifest_path.exists():\\n            raise FileNotFoundError(\\n                f\\\"No precomputed cache at {cache_dir}. \\\"\\n                f\\\"Run with --precompute first, or use --auto.\\\"\\n            )\\n        manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text())\\n        print(f\\\"[train] Loading cache ({bm}): {manifest['n_samples']} samples, \\\"\\n              f\\\"K={manifest['K']}, obs_dim={manifest['obs_dim']}\\\")\\n        all_obs_features.append(torch.load(cache_dir / \\\"obs_features.pt\\\", weights_only=True))\\n        all_k_samples.append(torch.load(cache_dir / \\\"k_samples.pt\\\", weights_only=True))\\n        all_expert_actions.append(torch.load(cache_dir / \\\"expert_actions.pt\\\", weights_only=True))\\n\\n    obs_features = torch.cat(all_obs_features)\\n    k_samples = torch.cat(all_k_samples)\\n    expert_actions = torch.cat(all_expert_actions)\\n    print(f\\\"[train] Total: {obs_features.shape[0]} samples from \\\"\\n          f\\\"{len(args.benchmark)} benchmark(s)\\\")\\n\\n    # Train/val split\\n    N = obs_features.shape[0]\\n    n_val = int(N * args.val_split)\\n    n_train = N - n_val\\n    perm = torch.randperm(N)\\n    train_idx, val_idx = perm[:n_train], perm[n_train:]\\n\\n    train_ds = CRMDataset(obs_features[train_idx], k_samples[train_idx],\\n                          expert_actions[train_idx])\\n    val_ds = CRMDataset(obs_features[val_idx], k_samples[val_idx],\\n                        expert_actions[val_idx])\\n\\n    train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=args.batch_size,\\n                              shuffle=True, num_workers=2, pin_memory=True)\\n    val_loader = DataLoader(val_ds, batch_size=args.batch_size * 2,\\n                            shuffle=False, num_workers=2, pin_memory=True)\\n\\n    # CGS + CRM\\n    cgs = ConformilizedGaussianScoring(\\n        K=manifest[\\\"K\\\"], horizon=H_EFF, action_dim=ACTION_DIM\\n    )\\n    crm = ConformalRefinementModule(\\n        action_dim=H_EFF * ACTION_DIM,  # 30\\n        uncertainty_dim=ACTION_DIM * H_EFF + H_EFF + H_EFF,  # 40\\n        obs_dim=manifest[\\\"obs_dim\\\"],\\n        hidden_dim=args.hidden_dim,\\n    ).to(device)\\n\\n    print(f\\\"[train] CRM parameters: {crm.count_parameters\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 252 字符] ...\\n\\nmax_keep=5)\\n\\n    noise_scale = torch.linspace(\\n        args.aug_noise_min, args.aug_noise_max, ACTION_DIM\\n    ).to(device)\\n\\n    best_val_loss = float(\\\"inf\\\")\\n\\n    for epoch in range(1, args.epochs + 1):\\n        # ── Train ──\\n        crm.train()\\n        epoch_loss = 0.0\\n        n_batches = 0\\n\\n        for batch_idx, batch in enumerate(train_loader):\\n            obs_feat = batch[\\\"obs_features\\\"].to(device)       # (B, 960)\\n            k_samp = batch[\\\"k_samples\\\"].to(device)             # (B, K, H, D)\\n            expert = batch[\\\"expert_action\\\"].to(device)         # (B, H, D)\\n            expert_flat = expert.reshape(expert.shape[0], -1)  # (B, 30)\\n\\n            # CGS\\n            uf, ma = cgs.compute_features_batch(k_samp)\\n            uf = uf.to(device)\\n            ma = ma.to(device)\\n\\n            # CRM forward\\n            refined = crm(ma, uf, obs_feat)\\n            loss = loss_fn(refined, expert_flat)\\n\\n            # Synthetic augmentation: perturb inputs, keep clean target\\n            n_aug = int(obs_feat.shape[0] * args.aug_ratio)\\n            if n_aug > 0:\\n                ma_noise = noise_scale.repeat(H_EFF)\\n                aug_ma = ma[:n_aug] + torch.randn_like(ma[:n_aug]) * ma_noise\\n                aug_uf = uf[:n_aug] + torch.randn_like(uf[:n_aug]) * noise_scale.mean() * 0.1\\n                aug_refined = crm(aug_ma, aug_uf, obs_feat[:n_aug])\\n                aug_loss = loss_fn(aug_refined, expert_flat[:n_aug])\\n                loss = loss + 0.5 * aug_loss\\n\\n            optimizer.zero_grad()\\n            loss.backward()\\n            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(crm.parameters(), 1.0)\\n            optimizer.step()\\n\\n            epoch_loss += loss.item()\\n            n_batches += 1\\n\\n            if (batch_idx + 1) % args.log_every == 0:\\n                print(f\\\"  epoch {epoch} batch {batch_idx+1}/{len(train_loader)} \\\"\\n                      f\\\"loss={loss.item():.6f}\\\")\\n\\n        scheduler.step()\\n        avg_train_loss = epoch_loss / max(n_batches, 1)\\n\\n        # ── Validate ──\\n        crm.eval()\\n        val_loss = 0.0\\n        n_val_batches = 0\\n        with torch.no_grad():\\n            for batch in val_loader:\\n                obs_feat = batch[\\\"obs_features\\\"].to(device)\\n                k_samp = batch[\\\"k_samples\\\"].to(device)\\n                expert = batch[\\\"expert_action\\\"].to(device)\\n                expert_flat = expert.reshape(expert.shape[0], -1)\\n\\n                uf, ma = cgs.compute_features_batch(k_samp)\\n                uf, ma = uf.to(device), ma.to(device)\\n\\n                refined = crm(ma, uf, obs_feat)\\n                val_loss += loss_fn(refined, expert_flat).item()\\n                n_val_batches += 1\\n\\n        avg_val_loss = val_loss / max(n_val_batches, 1)\\n\\n        print(f\\\"Epoch {epoch}/{args.epochs}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"train_loss={avg_train_loss:.6f}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"val_loss={avg_val_loss:.6f}  \\\"\\n              f\\\"lr={scheduler.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e}\\\")\\n\\n        metrics = {\\\"train_loss\\\": avg_train_loss, \\\"val_loss\\\": avg_val_loss}\\n        ckpt_mgr.save(crm, optimizer, epoch, metrics)\\n\\n        if avg_val_loss < best_val_loss:\\n            best_val_loss = avg_val_loss\\n            torch.save(crm.state_dict(),\\n                       Path(args.output_dir) / \\\"crm_best.pt\\\")\\n            print(f\\\"  → New best val_loss: {best_val_loss:.6f}\\\")\\n\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n[train] Done. Best val_loss={best_val_loss:.6f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"[train] Checkpoints at {args.output_dir}\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    args = parse_args()\\n    print(f\\\"[train_crm] args: {vars(args)}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"[train_crm] device: {'cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu'}\\\")\\n\\n    if args.precompute:\\n        precompute_features(args)\\n        return\\n\\n    if args.auto:\\n        needs_precompute = any(\\n            not (Path(args.cache_dir) / bm / \\\"manifest.json\\\").exists()\\n            for bm in args.benchmark\\n        )\\n        if needs_precompute:\\n            print(\\\"[auto] Cache not found for some benchmarks, precomputing...\\\")\\n            precompute_features(args)\\n\\n    train(args)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.31}}"}}}

weights_only=True))\\\\n\\\\n    obs_features = torch.cat(all_obs_features)\\\\n    k_samples = torch.cat(all_k_samples)\\\\n    expert_actions = torch.cat(all_expert_actions)\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"[train] Total: {obs_features.shape[0]} samples from \\\\\\\"\\\\n          f\\\\\\\"{len(args.benchmark)} benchmark(s)\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    # Train/val split\\\\n    N = train_ds = CRMDataset(obs_features[train_idx], k_samples[train_idx],\\\\n                          expert_actions[train_idx])\\\\n    val_ds = CRMDataset(obs_features[val_idx], k_samples[val_idx],\\\\n                        expert_actions[val_idx])\\\\n\\\\n    train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=args.batch_size,\\\\n                              shuffle=True, Insert preprocess_obs_batch before encode_prefix\nnew_method1 = '''    @torch.no_grad()\n    def preprocess_obs_batch(self, batch_obs):\n        dev = self.device\n        B = len(batch_obs)\n\n        agentview_tensors = torch.stack(\n            [self.img_transform(s[\"agentview_rgb\"]) for s in batch_obs]\n        ).to(dev)\n        imgs = [agentview_tensors]\n        img_masks = [torch.ones(B, dtype=torch.bool, device=dev)]\n\n        if batch_obs[0].get(\"eye_in_hand_rgb\") is not None:\n            eih_tensors = torch.stack(\n                [self.img_transform(s[\"eye_in_hand_rgb\"]) for s in batch_obs]\n            ).to(dev)\n            imgs.append(eih_tensors)\n            img_masks.append(torch.ones(B, dtype=torch.bool, device=dev))\n\n        tokens = self.tokenizer(\n            [s[\"task_name\"] for s in batch_obs],\n            return_tensors=\"pt\",\n            padding=\"max_length\",\n            max_length=self.config.tokenizer_max_length,\n            truncation=True,\n        )\n        lang_tokens = tokens[\"input_ids\"].to(dev)\n        lang_masks = tokens[\"attention_mask\"].to(dev).bool()\n\n        state = torch.zeros(B, self.config.max_state_dim, device=dev, dtype=torch.float32)\n        for i, s in enumerate(batch_obs):\n            js = s[\"joint_states\"]\n            state[i, :js.shape[0]] = torch.tensor(js, dtype=torch.float32)\n\n        return imgs, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state\n\n'''\n\nmarker1 = '    @torch.no_grad()\\n    def encode_prefix('\nassert marker1 in content, \"Could not find encode_prefix method\"\ncontent = content.replace(marker1, new_method1 + marker1)\n\n# 2. Append batch get method at end of class\nnew_method2 = '''\n    @torch.no_grad()\n    def get_obs_features_and_samples_batch(self, batch_obs, K=10):\n        imgs, img_masks, lt, lm, state = self.preprocess_obs_batch(batch_obs)\n        obs_feat, kv_cache, pad_masks = self.encode_prefix(\n            imgs, img_masks, lt, lm, state\n        )\n        samples = self.generate_k_samples(kv_cache, pad_masks, K=K)\n        return obs_feat, samples\n'''\n\ncontent = content.rstrip() + '\\n' + new_method2 + '\\n'\n\nwith open(path, 'w') as f:\n    f.write(content)\nprint(\"OK: smolvla_wrapper.py updated\")\nPYEOF","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3460,"cache_read_input_tokens":49833,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3460},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0672f7e2-0e43-4c1f-a727-2021f7e06766","uuid":"9282b673-b5b2-4837-a44e-480ce6005676"}
